177                                 OP_R_TYPE: begin
178        0/1     ==>                  if ((EXTENSION_M && funct7 == 7'b0000001) || 
179                                         (funct7 == 7'b0000000) || 
180                                         (funct7 == 7'b0100000 && (funct3 == 3'b000 || funct3 == 3'b101))) begin
181        0/1     ==>                      reg_write    = 1'b1;
182        0/1     ==>                      result_sel   = 2'b00;
183        0/1     ==>                      mem_write    = 1'b0;
184        0/1     ==>                      mem_read     = 1'b0;
185        0/1     ==>                      alu_src      = 1'b0;
186        0/1     ==>                      pc_src_auipc = 1'b0;
187        0/1     ==>                      imm_sel      = 3'b000;
188        0/1     ==>                      branch       = 1'b0;
189        0/1     ==>                      jump         = 1'b0;
190        0/1     ==>                      alu_op_type  = 2'b10;
191        0/1     ==>                      mem_size     = 3'b000;
192                                     end else begin
193        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
194        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
195                                     end

198                                 OP_I_TYPE: begin
199        0/1     ==>                  if ((funct3 == 3'b001 && funct7 != 7'b0000000) ||

201        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
202        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
203                                     end else begin

211        0/1     ==>                      branch       = 1'b0;
212        0/1     ==>                      jump         = 1'b0;

226        0/1     ==>                  jump         = 1'b0;

228        0/1     ==>                  mem_size     = funct3;
229        0/1     ==>                  result_sel = 2'b01;

244                                 end

246                                 OP_BRANCH: begin
247        0/1     ==>                  if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
248        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
249        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
250                                     end else begin
251        0/1     ==>                      reg_write    = 1'b0;
252        0/1     ==>                      result_sel   = 2'b00;
253        0/1     ==>                      mem_write    = 1'b0;
254        0/1     ==>                      mem_read     = 1'b0;
255        0/1     ==>                      alu_src      = 1'b0;
256        0/1     ==>                      pc_src_auipc = 1'b0;
257        0/1     ==>                      imm_sel      = 3'b010;
258        0/1     ==>                      branch       = 1'b1;
259        0/1     ==>                      jump         = 1'b0;
260        0/1     ==>                      alu_op_type  = 2'b01;
261        0/1     ==>                      mem_size     = 3'b000;

269        0/1     ==>                  mem_read     = 1'b0;

272        0/1     ==>                  imm_sel      = 3'b100;
273        0/1     ==>                  branch       = 1'b0;

312        0/1     ==>                  alu_src      = 1'b1;

314        0/1     ==>                  imm_sel      = 3'b011;
315        0/1     ==>                  branch       = 1'b0;
316        0/1     ==>                  jump         = 1'b0;
317        0/1     ==>                  alu_op_type  = 2'b00;

321                                 OP_AMO: begin
322        0/1     ==>                  if (EXTENSION_A) begin
323        0/1     ==>                      if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
324        0/1     ==>                          amo_en = 1'b1;
325        0/1     ==>                          amo_op = funct7[6:2];
326        0/1     ==>                          mem_size = funct3;
327        0/1     ==>                          if (amo_op == 5'b00010) begin // LR
328        0/1     ==>                              mem_read   = 1'b1;
329        0/1     ==>                              mem_write  = 1'b0;
330        0/1     ==>                              reg_write  = 1'b1;
331        0/1     ==>                              result_sel = 2'b01;
332        0/1     ==>                          end else if (amo_op == 5'b00011) begin // SC
333        0/1     ==>                              mem_read   = 1'b0;
334        0/1     ==>                              mem_write  = 1'b1;
335        0/1     ==>                              reg_write  = 1'b1;
336        0/1     ==>                              result_sel = 2'b01;
337                                             end else begin // Other AMOs
338        0/1     ==>                              mem_read   = 1'b1;
339        0/1     ==>                              mem_write  = 1'b1;
340        0/1     ==>                              reg_write  = 1'b1;
341        0/1     ==>                              result_sel = 2'b01;
342                                             end
343                                         end else begin
344        0/1     ==>                          exception = 1'b1;
345        0/1     ==>                          exception_cause = 4'd2;
346                                         end
347                                     end else begin
348        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
349        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
350                                     end
351                                 end
352                     
353                                 OP_FENCE: begin
354                                     // NOP - all defaults (zeros)
355                                 end
356                     
357                                 OP_SYSTEM: begin
358        1/1                          if (PRIVILEGED) begin
359        1/1                              if (funct3 == 3'b000) begin
360        0/1     ==>                          if (funct7 == 7'b0000000 && rs2_addr == 5'b00000) begin
361                                                 // ECALL
362        0/1     ==>                              exception = 1'b1;
363        0/1     ==>                              exception_cause = 4'd11;
364        0/1     ==>                          end else if (funct7 == 7'b0000000 && rs2_addr == 5'b00001) begin
365                                                 // EBREAK
366        0/1     ==>                              exception = 1'b1;
367        0/1     ==>                              exception_cause = 4'd3;
368        0/1     ==>                          end else if (funct7 == 7'b0011000 && rs2_addr == 5'b00010) begin
369                                                 // MRET
370        0/1     ==>                              mret_exec = 1'b1;
371                                             end else begin
372        0/1     ==>                              exception = 1'b1;
373        0/1     ==>                              exception_cause = 4'd2;
374                                             end
375                                         end else begin
376                                             // Zicsr
377        1/1                                  reg_write   = 1'b1;
378        1/1                                  result_sel  = 2'b11;
379        1/1                                  csr_imm_sel = funct3[2];
380                                             
381        1/1                                  if (funct3[2]) begin
382        0/1     ==>                              imm_sel = 3'b101; // IMM_Z
383                                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
384                                             
385        1/1                                  case (funct3[1:0])
386                                                 2'b01: begin // CSRRW / CSRRWI
387        0/1     ==>                                  csr_op = 2'b01;
388        0/1     ==>                                  csr_write = 1'b1;
389                                                 end
390                                                 2'b10: begin // CSRRS / CSRRSI
391        1/1                                          csr_op = 2'b10;
392        1/1                                          csr_write = (rs1_addr != 5'b00000);
393                                                 end
394                                                 2'b11: begin // CSRRC / CSRRCI
395        0/1     ==>                                  csr_op = 2'b11;
396        0/1     ==>                                  csr_write = (rs1_addr != 5'b00000);
397                                                 end
398                                                 default: begin
399        0/1     ==>                                  csr_op = 2'b00;
400        0/1     ==>                                  csr_write = 1'b0;
401        0/1     ==>                                  exception = 1'b1;
402        0/1     ==>                                  exception_cause = 4'd2;
403                                                 end
404                                             endcase
405                                         end
406                                     end else begin
407        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
408        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
409                                     end
410                                 end
411                     
412                                 OP_LOAD_FP: begin
413        1/1                          if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
414        1/1                              if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
415        0/1     ==>                          mem_read     = 1'b1;
416        0/1     ==>                          fp_we        = 1'b1;
417        0/1     ==>                          fp_mem_read  = 1'b1;
418        0/1     ==>                          alu_src      = 1'b1;

420        0/1     ==>                          alu_op_type  = 2'b00;  // ADD
421        0/1     ==>                          mem_size     = funct3;

423                                         end else begin
424        1/1                                  exception = 1'b1;
425        1/1                                  exception_cause = 4'd2;
426                                         end
427                                     end else begin
428        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
429        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
430                                     end
431        1/1                          result_sel = 2'b01;
432                                 end
433                     
434                                 OP_STORE_FP: begin
435        0/1     ==>                  if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
436        0/1     ==>                      if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
437        0/1     ==>                          mem_write    = 1'b1;
438        0/1     ==>                          fp_mem_write = 1'b1;
439        0/1     ==>                          alu_src      = 1'b1;
440        0/1     ==>                          imm_sel      = 3'b001; // IMM_S
441        0/1     ==>                          alu_op_type  = 2'b00;  // ADD
442        0/1     ==>                          mem_size     = funct3;
443                                         end else begin
444        0/1     ==>                          exception = 1'b1;
445        0/1     ==>                          exception_cause = 4'd2;
446                                         end
447                                     end else begin
448        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
449        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
450                                     end
451                                 end
452                     
453                                 OP_FMADD: begin
454        0/1     ==>                  if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
455        0/1     ==>                      fp_we        = 1'b1;
456        0/1     ==>                      fmt          = funct7[1:0];
457        0/1     ==>                      rm           = funct3;
458        0/1     ==>                      fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
459        0/1     ==>                      fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMADD;
460                                     end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
461                                 end
462                     
463                                 OP_FMSUB: begin
464        1/1                          if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
465        1/1                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
466        1/1                              fmt          = funct7[1:0];
467        1/1                              rm           = funct3;
468        1/1                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
469        1/1                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMSUB;
470                                     end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
471                                 end
472                     
473                                 OP_FNMSUB: begin
474        0/1     ==>                  if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
475        0/1     ==>                      fp_we        = 1'b1;
476        0/1     ==>                      fmt          = funct7[1:0];
477        0/1     ==>                      rm           = funct3;
478        0/1     ==>                      fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
479        0/1     ==>                      fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FNMSUB;
480                                     end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
481                                 end
482                     
483                                 OP_FNMADD: begin
484        0/1     ==>                  if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
485        0/1     ==>                      fp_we        = 1'b1;
486        0/1     ==>                      fmt          = funct7[1:0];
487        0/1     ==>                      rm           = funct3;
488        0/1     ==>                      fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
489        0/1     ==>                      fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FNMADD;
490                                     end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
491                                 end
492                     
493                                 OP_FP: begin
494        0/1     ==>                  if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
495        0/1     ==>                      fmt = funct7[1:0];
496        0/1     ==>                      rm  = funct3;
497        0/1     ==>                      case (funct7[6:2])
498                                             5'b00000: begin // FADD
499        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
500        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FADD;
501        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
502                                             end
503                                             5'b00001: begin // FSUB
504        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
505        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FSUB;
506        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
507                                             end
508                                             5'b00010: begin // FMUL
509        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
510        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMUL;
511        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
512                                             end
513                                             5'b00011: begin // FDIV
514        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
515        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FDIV;
516        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
517                                             end
518                                             5'b01011: begin // FSQRT
519        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
520        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FSQRT;
521        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
522                                             end
523                                             5'b00100: begin // FSGNJ
524        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
525        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FSGNJ;
526                                             end
527                                             5'b00101: begin // FMIN/MAX
528        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
529        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMIN;
530        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
531                                             end
532                                             5'b01000: begin // FCVT.S.D/D.S
533        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
534        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCVT;
535        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
536                                             end
537                                             5'b10100: begin // FCMP
538        0/1     ==>                              reg_write    = 1'b1;
539        0/1     ==>                              fp_to_int    = 1'b1;
540        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCMP;
541        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
542                                             end
543                                             5'b11000: begin // FCVT.W.S/D
544        0/1     ==>                              reg_write    = 1'b1;
545        0/1     ==>                              fp_to_int    = 1'b1;
546        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCVT;
547        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
548                                             end
549                                             5'b11010: begin // FCVT.S/D.W
550        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
551        0/1     ==>                              int_to_fp    = 1'b1;
552        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCVT;
553        0/1     ==>                              fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
554                                             end
555                                             5'b11100: begin // FMV.X.W/D or FCLASS
556        0/1     ==>                              reg_write    = 1'b1;
557        0/1     ==>                              fp_to_int    = 1'b1;
558        0/1     ==>                              if (funct3 == 3'b001) begin
559        0/1     ==>                                  fp_alu_op = FP_ALU_FCLASS;
560                                                 end else begin
561        0/1     ==>                                  fp_alu_op = FP_ALU_FMV;
562                                                 end
563                                             end
564                                             5'b11110: begin // FMV.W/D.X
565        0/1     ==>                              fp_we        = 1'b1;
566        0/1     ==>                              int_to_fp    = 1'b1;
567        0/1     ==>                              fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMV;
568                                             end
569                                             default: begin
570        0/1     ==>                              exception = 1'b1;
571        0/1     ==>                              exception_cause = 4'd2;
572                                             end
573                                         endcase
574                                     end else begin
575        0/1     ==>                      exception = 1'b1;
576        0/1     ==>                      exception_cause = 4'd2;
577                                     end
578                                 end
579                     
580                                 default: begin
581                                     // Unknown opcode - safe defaults (all zeros)
582        1/1                          exception = 1'b1;
583        1/1                          exception_cause = 4'd2;
584                                 end
585                             endcase
586                     
587        1/1                  if (opcode == OP_LOAD_FP || opcode == OP_STORE_FP || opcode == OP_FMADD || opcode == OP_FMSUB || opcode == OP_FNMSUB || opcode == OP_FNMADD || opcode == OP_FP) begin
588        1/1                      if (mstatus_fs == 2'b00) begin
589        1/1                          exception = 1'b1;
590        1/1                          exception_cause = 4'd2;
591        0/1     ==>              end else if (opcode != OP_LOAD_FP && opcode != OP_STORE_FP && (funct3 == 3'b101 || funct3 == 3'b110)) begin
592        0/1     ==>                  exception = 1'b1;
593        0/1     ==>                  exception_cause = 4'd2;
594        0/1     ==>              end else if (opcode != OP_LOAD_FP && opcode != OP_STORE_FP && funct3 == 3'b111 && fcsr_rm >= 3'b101) begin
595        0/1     ==>                  exception = 1'b1;
596        0/1     ==>                  exception_cause = 4'd2;
597                                 end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
598        1/1                      result_sel = 2'b01;
599                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
600                         end
601                     
602                         //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
603                         // ALU Decoder: Generates alu_op based on alu_op_type, funct3, funct7[5]
604                         //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
605                         always @(*) begin
606                             // Default ALU operation
607        1/1                  alu_op = ALU_ADD;
608                     
609                             // Special case: LUI forces PASS_B regardless of alu_op_type
610        1/1                  if (opcode == OP_LUI) begin
611        0/1     ==>              alu_op = ALU_PASS_B;
612                             end
613        1/1                  else if (opcode == OP_AMO) begin
614        0/1     ==>              alu_op = ALU_PASS_A;
615                             end
616                             else begin
617        1/1                      case (alu_op_type)
618                                     2'b00: begin
619                                         // Fixed ADD (Load, Store, JALR, JAL, AUIPC)
620        1/1                              alu_op = ALU_ADD;
621                                     end
622                     
623                                     2'b01: begin
624                                         // Branch comparison
625        0/1     ==>                      case (funct3)
626        0/1     ==>                          3'b000:  alu_op = ALU_SUB;   // BEQ
627        0/1     ==>                          3'b001:  alu_op = ALU_SUB;   // BNE
628        0/1     ==>                          3'b100:  alu_op = ALU_SLT;   // BLT
629        0/1     ==>                          3'b101:  alu_op = ALU_SLT;   // BGE
630        0/1     ==>                          3'b110:  alu_op = ALU_SLTU;  // BLTU
631        0/1     ==>                          3'b111:  alu_op = ALU_SLTU;  // BGEU

260                            alu_op_type  = 2'b01;
261                            mem_size     = 3'b000;
262                        end
263                    end
264        
265                    OP_JAL: begin
266                        reg_write    = 1'b1;
                           ==>
267                        result_sel   = 2'b10;
268                        mem_write    = 1'b0;
269                        mem_read     = 1'b0;
270                        alu_src      = 1'b0;
271                        pc_src_auipc = 1'b0;
272                        imm_sel      = 3'b100;
273                        branch       = 1'b0;
274                        jump         = 1'b1;
275                        alu_op_type  = 2'b00;
276                        mem_size     = 3'b000;
277                    end
278        
279                    OP_JALR: begin
280                        reg_write    = 1'b1;
                           ==>
281                        result_sel   = 2'b10;
282                        mem_write    = 1'b0;
283                        mem_read     = 1'b0;
284                        alu_src      = 1'b1;
285                        pc_src_auipc = 1'b0;
286                        imm_sel      = 3'b000;
287                        branch       = 1'b0;
288                        jump         = 1'b1;
289                        alu_op_type  = 2'b00;
290                        mem_size     = 3'b000;
291                    end
292        
293                    OP_LUI: begin
294                        reg_write    = 1'b1;
                           ==>
295                        result_sel   = 2'b00;
296                        mem_write    = 1'b0;
297                        mem_read     = 1'b0;
298                        alu_src      = 1'b1;
299                        pc_src_auipc = 1'b0;
300                        imm_sel      = 3'b011;
301                        branch       = 1'b0;
302                        jump         = 1'b0;
303                        alu_op_type  = 2'b00;
304                        mem_size     = 3'b000;
305                    end
306        
307                    OP_AUIPC: begin
308                        reg_write    = 1'b1;
                           ==>
309                        result_sel   = 2'b00;
310                        mem_write    = 1'b0;
311                        mem_read     = 1'b0;
312                        alu_src      = 1'b1;
313                        pc_src_auipc = 1'b1;
314                        imm_sel      = 3'b011;
315                        branch       = 1'b0;
316                        jump         = 1'b0;
317                        alu_op_type  = 2'b00;
318                        mem_size     = 3'b000;
319                    end
320        
321                    OP_AMO: begin
322                        if (EXTENSION_A) begin
                           -5-  
323                            if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
                               -6-  
324                                amo_en = 1'b1;
325                                amo_op = funct7[6:2];
326                                mem_size = funct3;
327                                if (amo_op == 5'b00010) begin // LR
                                   -7-  
328                                    mem_read   = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
329                                    mem_write  = 1'b0;
330                                    reg_write  = 1'b1;
331                                    result_sel = 2'b01;
332                                end else if (amo_op == 5'b00011) begin // SC
                                            -8-  
333                                    mem_read   = 1'b0;
                                       ==>
334                                    mem_write  = 1'b1;
335                                    reg_write  = 1'b1;
336                                    result_sel = 2'b01;
337                                end else begin // Other AMOs
338                                    mem_read   = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
339                                    mem_write  = 1'b1;
340                                    reg_write  = 1'b1;
341                                    result_sel = 2'b01;
342                                end
343                            end else begin
344                                exception = 1'b1;
                                   ==>
345                                exception_cause = 4'd2;
346                            end
347                        end else begin
348                            exception = 1'b1;
                               ==>
349                            exception_cause = 4'd2;
350                        end
351                    end
352        
353                    OP_FENCE: begin
                       ==>
354                        // NOP - all defaults (zeros)
355                    end
356        
357                    OP_SYSTEM: begin
358                        if (PRIVILEGED) begin
                           -9-  
359                            if (funct3 == 3'b000) begin
                               -10-  
360                                if (funct7 == 7'b0000000 && rs2_addr == 5'b00000) begin
                                   -11-  
361                                    // ECALL
362                                    exception = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
363                                    exception_cause = 4'd11;
364                                end else if (funct7 == 7'b0000000 && rs2_addr == 5'b00001) begin
                                            -12-  
365                                    // EBREAK
366                                    exception = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
367                                    exception_cause = 4'd3;
368                                end else if (funct7 == 7'b0011000 && rs2_addr == 5'b00010) begin
                                            -13-  
369                                    // MRET
370                                    mret_exec = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
371                                end else begin
372                                    exception = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
373                                    exception_cause = 4'd2;
374                                end
375                            end else begin
376                                // Zicsr
377                                reg_write   = 1'b1;
378                                result_sel  = 2'b11;
379                                csr_imm_sel = funct3[2];
380                                
381                                if (funct3[2]) begin
                                   -14-  
382                                    imm_sel = 3'b101; // IMM_Z
                                       ==>
383                                end
                                   MISSING_ELSE
                                   ==>
384                                
385                                case (funct3[1:0])
                                   -15-  
386                                    2'b01: begin // CSRRW / CSRRWI
387                                        csr_op = 2'b01;
                                           ==>
388                                        csr_write = 1'b1;
389                                    end
390                                    2'b10: begin // CSRRS / CSRRSI
391                                        csr_op = 2'b10;
                                           ==>
392                                        csr_write = (rs1_addr != 5'b00000);
393                                    end
394                                    2'b11: begin // CSRRC / CSRRCI
395                                        csr_op = 2'b11;
                                           ==>
396                                        csr_write = (rs1_addr != 5'b00000);
397                                    end
398                                    default: begin
399                                        csr_op = 2'b00;
                                           ==>
400                                        csr_write = 1'b0;
401                                        exception = 1'b1;
402                                        exception_cause = 4'd2;
403                                    end
404                                endcase
405                            end
406                        end else begin
407                            exception = 1'b1;
                               ==>
408                            exception_cause = 4'd2;
409                        end
410                    end
411        
412                    OP_LOAD_FP: begin
413                        if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
                           -16-  
414                            if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
                               -17-  
415                                mem_read     = 1'b1;
416                                fp_we        = 1'b1;
417                                fp_mem_read  = 1'b1;
418                                alu_src      = 1'b1;
419                                imm_sel      = 3'b000; // IMM_I
420                                alu_op_type  = 2'b00;  // ADD
421                                mem_size     = funct3;
422                                fmt          = (funct3 == 3'b011) ? 2'b01 : 2'b00;
                                                                     -18-  
                                                                     ==>  
                                                                     ==>  
423                            end else begin
424                                exception = 1'b1;
                                   ==>
425                                exception_cause = 4'd2;
426                            end
427                        end else begin
428                            exception = 1'b1;
                               ==>
429                            exception_cause = 4'd2;
430                        end
431                        result_sel = 2'b01;
432                    end
433        
434                    OP_STORE_FP: begin
435                        if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
                           -19-  
436                            if (funct3 == 3'b010 || funct3 == 3'b011) begin
                               -20-  
437                                mem_write    = 1'b1;
                                   ==>
438                                fp_mem_write = 1'b1;
439                                alu_src      = 1'b1;
440                                imm_sel      = 3'b001; // IMM_S
441                                alu_op_type  = 2'b00;  // ADD
442                                mem_size     = funct3;
443                            end else begin
444                                exception = 1'b1;
                                   ==>
445                                exception_cause = 4'd2;
446                            end
447                        end else begin
448                            exception = 1'b1;
                               ==>
449                            exception_cause = 4'd2;
450                        end
451                    end
452        
453                    OP_FMADD: begin
454                        if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
                           -21-  
455                            fp_we        = 1'b1;
                               ==>
456                            fmt          = funct7[1:0];
457                            rm           = funct3;
458                            fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
459                            fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMADD;
460                        end
                           MISSING_ELSE
                           ==>
461                    end

463                    OP_FMSUB: begin
464                        if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
                           -22-  
465                            fp_we        = 1'b1;
                               ==>

467                            rm           = funct3;

470                        end

                           -23-

                               ==>

                           -24-

                               ==>

493                    OP_FP: begin
494                        if (EXTENSION_F || EXTENSION_D) begin
                           -25-  
495                            fmt = funct7[1:0];
496                            rm  = funct3;
497                            case (funct7[6:2])
                               -26-  
498                                5'b00000: begin // FADD
499                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
500                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FADD;
501                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
502                                end
503                                5'b00001: begin // FSUB
504                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
505                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FSUB;
506                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
507                                end
508                                5'b00010: begin // FMUL
509                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
510                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMUL;
511                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
512                                end
513                                5'b00011: begin // FDIV
514                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
515                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FDIV;
516                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
517                                end
518                                5'b01011: begin // FSQRT
519                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
520                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FSQRT;
521                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
522                                end
523                                5'b00100: begin // FSGNJ
524                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
525                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FSGNJ;
526                                end
527                                5'b00101: begin // FMIN/MAX
528                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
529                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMIN;
530                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
531                                end
532                                5'b01000: begin // FCVT.S.D/D.S
533                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
534                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCVT;
535                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
536                                end
537                                5'b10100: begin // FCMP
538                                    reg_write    = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
539                                    fp_to_int    = 1'b1;
540                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCMP;
541                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
542                                end
543                                5'b11000: begin // FCVT.W.S/D
544                                    reg_write    = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
545                                    fp_to_int    = 1'b1;
546                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCVT;
547                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
548                                end
549                                5'b11010: begin // FCVT.S/D.W
550                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
551                                    int_to_fp    = 1'b1;
552                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FCVT;
553                                    fp_fflags_we = 1'b1;
554                                end
555                                5'b11100: begin // FMV.X.W/D or FCLASS
556                                    reg_write    = 1'b1;
557                                    fp_to_int    = 1'b1;
558                                    if (funct3 == 3'b001) begin
                                       -27-  
559                                        fp_alu_op = FP_ALU_FCLASS;
                                           ==>
560                                    end else begin
561                                        fp_alu_op = FP_ALU_FMV;
                                           ==>
562                                    end
563                                end
564                                5'b11110: begin // FMV.W/D.X
565                                    fp_we        = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
566                                    int_to_fp    = 1'b1;
567                                    fp_alu_op    = FP_ALU_FMV;
568                                end
569                                default: begin
570                                    exception = 1'b1;
                                       ==>
571                                    exception_cause = 4'd2;
572                                end
573                            endcase
574                        end else begin
575                            exception = 1'b1;
                               ==>
576                            exception_cause = 4'd2;
577                        end
578                    end
579        
580                    default: begin
581                        // Unknown opcode - safe defaults (all zeros)
582                        exception = 1'b1;
                           ==>

635                         );
636                     
637                     // CDB Broadcast
638                         reg [3:0] rob_idx_ex, rob_idx_mem, rob_idx_wb;
639                         reg valid_ex, valid_mem, valid_wb;
640                         
641                         reg cdb_done_mem, cdb_done_wb;
642                     
643                         wire ex_ready = !(mem_read_ex || mem_write_ex || fp_mem_read_ex || fp_mem_write_ex || csr_write_ex || result_sel_ex == 2'b11 || amo_en_ex);
644                         wire ex_wants_cdb = valid_ex && (ex_ready || exception_ex);
645                         wire mem_ready = !(mem_read_mem || fp_mem_read_mem || csr_write_mem || result_sel_mem == 2'b11 || amo_en_mem);
646                         wire mem_wants_cdb = valid_mem && ((!cdb_done_mem && mem_ready) || exception_mem);
647                         wire wb_wants_cdb = valid_wb && (!cdb_done_wb || exception_wb);
648                     
649                         wire grant_wb  = wb_wants_cdb;
650                         wire grant_mem = mem_wants_cdb && !grant_wb;
651                         wire grant_ex  = ex_wants_cdb && !grant_wb && !grant_mem;
652                     
653                         always @(posedge global_gated_clk or negedge rst_n) begin
654        1/1                  if (!rst_n) begin
655        1/1                      valid_ex <= 0;
656        1/1                      valid_mem <= 0;
657        1/1                      valid_wb <= 0;
658        1/1                      exception_wb <= 0;
659        1/1                      exception_cause_wb <= 0;
660        1/1                      pc_sel_mem <= 0;
661        1/1                      pc_sel_wb <= 0;
662        1/1                      pc_target_mem <= 0;
663        1/1                      pc_target_wb <= 0;
664        1/1                      rob_idx_ex <= 0;
665        1/1                      rob_idx_mem <= 0;
666        1/1                      rob_idx_wb <= 0;
667        1/1                      cdb_done_mem <= 0;
668        1/1                      cdb_done_wb <= 0;
669                             end else begin
670        1/1                      if (flush_id_ex | final_flush) begin
671        1/1                          valid_ex <= 0;
672                                 end else begin
673        1/1                          valid_ex <= dispatch_en;
674        1/1                          rob_idx_ex <= rob_idx_disp;
675                                 end
676                     
677        1/1                      if (flush_ex_mem | final_flush) begin
678        1/1                          valid_mem <= 0;
679        1/1                          cdb_done_mem <= 0;
680        1/1                          pc_sel_mem <= 0;
681        1/1                          pc_target_mem <= 0;
682                                 end else begin
683        1/1                          valid_mem <= valid_ex;
684        1/1                          rob_idx_mem <= rob_idx_ex;
685        1/1                          cdb_done_mem <= grant_ex;
686        1/1                          pc_sel_mem <= pc_sel_ex;
687        1/1                          pc_target_mem <= pc_target_ex;
688                                 end
689                     
690        1/1                      if (flush_mem_wb | final_flush) begin
691        1/1                          valid_wb <= 0;
692        1/1                          cdb_done_wb <= 0;
693        1/1                          exception_wb <= 0;
694        1/1                          exception_cause_wb <= 0;
695        1/1                          pc_sel_wb <= 0;
696        1/1                          pc_target_wb <= 0;
697                                 end else begin
698        1/1                          valid_wb <= valid_mem;
699        1/1                          rob_idx_wb <= rob_idx_mem;
700        1/1                          cdb_done_wb <= cdb_done_mem | grant_mem;
701        1/1                          exception_wb <= exception_mem;
702        1/1                          exception_cause_wb <= exception_cause_mem;
703        1/1                          pc_sel_wb <= pc_sel_mem;
704        1/1                          pc_target_wb <= pc_target_mem;

42                          assign rs1_data = (rs1_addr == 5'b0) ? 32'b0 : (we && (rd_addr == rs1_addr)) ? rd_data : regs[rs1_addr];

45                          //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
46                          // Synchronous Write Port (rising clock edge)
47                          // - On reset (rst_n == 0): all registers are cleared to zero
48                          // - On normal operation: write rd_data to regs[rd_addr] if we is asserted
49                          //   and rd_addr is not x0 (writes to x0 are silently ignored)
